Please read this Policy carefully before using the Services. Please contact the Company if you have any question. <br />\n<br />\n1.\tUser information to be collected and the method of collection<br />\n<br />\nIn this Policy, “User Information” means personal information (such as name, email address and telephone number) that will be saved as account information when registering an account in order to access the Services and which is collected by the Company in accordance with this Policy. “User Information” also includes information collected by the Company when the sharing function offered by the Company is used – for example, when contents are played, a website which uses the Company’s advertisement service is accessed or the Company’s advertisements or contents are displayed or operated, and other information set out below.<br />\n<br />\n(1)\tDevice information<br />\n<br />\nThe Company collects device-specific information (such as hardware model, version of the operating system being used, IP specific to the device, mobile network information such as telephone number). The Company may associate your account with your device ID and telephone number.<br />\n<br />\n(2)\tLocation information<br />\n<br />\nWhen you use the Services, the Company may collect information relating to your location. The location will be determined using various technologies including IP address, GPS and other sensors that provide the Company with information on nearby devices, Wi-Fi access point and cell towers.<br />\n<br />\n(3)\tCookies and similar technologies<br />\n<br />\nWhen you use the Services, the Company and its partners use various technologies to collect information. In doing so, your browsers and device may be identified through the use of Cookies and other similar technologies. When you use a service provided by the Company to its partner, the Company may collect and store certain information by using Cookies and other similar technologies. The Company enables other corporations and website owners to analyse the traffic to their websites and applications and, by using the Company’s technology, such information can be linked to information relating to number of visits to multiple websites. <br />\n<br />\n(4)\tAccount related information<br />\n<br />\nIn addition to information relating to you obtained from the Company’s partners, any information collected while you are signed-in to your account may be associated with your account. Please refer to paragraph 5 of this Policy for details on how to access, manage or delete information associated with your account.<br /> \n<br />\n2.\tPurpose of use<br />\n<br />\nThe User Information will be used for the purposes of providing, maintaining, protecting and improving the Services, developing a new service and protecting the Company and its users.<br />\n<br />\nThe specific purposes of use of User Information in connection with the provision of Services are as follows:<br />\n <br />\n(1)\tto provide contents tailored for the users (such as providing search results and advertisements that are more relevant); <br />\n(2)\tto deliver and display advertisements and contents based on attribute information, device information, location information and activity information; <br />\n(3)\tto improve the service and develop a new service; <br />\n(4)\tto generate statistic data in the form by which the specific individual cannot be   identified in order to utilize for marketing activity performed by the Company and/or a third party to be designated by the Company; and<br />\n(5)\t   to utilize to perform the above-mentioned purposes.<br />\n<br />\n3.\tUse of User Information<br />\n<br />\nIf the other users have already obtained other information to identify you, the Company may provide such other users with the profile information (such as your name and photographs) published by you.<br />\n<br />\nIf you use your account, your profile name, profile photo, activities on third party applications that are associated with the Company or your account (such as comments posted by you) may be displayed on the Services (including advertisements and other commercial displays). The Company will, however, respect the choice made by you with respect to the sharing and privacy setting on your account.<br />\n<br />\nIf you contact the Company, the Company will keep the communication records to help solve the issues you have. The Company may use your email address to send information regarding the Company’s Services (such as notice of proposed changes or improvements). <br />\n<br />\nThe Company will use the information collected through technologies such as Cookies to improve the user experience and the quality of the Company’s overall Services. For this purpose, the Company will save your language setting and display the Services in the language selected by you. When advertisements are customized and displayed for you, the identifying information obtained from Cookies and other similar technologies will not be associated with categories of information which are highly confidential (such as race, religion, sexuality and health).<br />\n<br />\nThe Company’s automated system will analyse your contents (including emails) and provide you with service functions which are more relevant to you. Such functions will include customized search results, customized advertisements and detection of spams and malware.<br />\n<br />\nBy associating the User Information obtained from certain services of the Company with information obtained from other services of the Company (such as User Information), the sharing of information with an acquaintance, for example, may become easier. Further, depending on the account setting, your activities on other websites and applications may be associated with your User Information to improve the Services or the advertisements by the Company. <br />\n<br />\n4.\tObtaining consent and request to cease use<br />\n<br />\nThe Company will always obtain your consent if it wishes to use your User Information for purposes other than those set out in this Policy. <br />\n<br />\nYou may request cessation of use of all or part of such User Information by giving notice to the Company. In such case, the Company will promptly cease the use of such User Information in accordance with the terms prescribed by the Company. However, as the collection or use of certain categories of User Information is a prerequisite for the Services, the Company will cease the collection of such User Information only when you have unsubscribed from the Services in accordance with the method prescribed by the Company.<br />\n<br />\n5.\tManagement of User Information<br />\n<br />\nThe Company aims to clearly set out the information that it collects and enable you to appropriately select how such information is used. For example, you are able to do the following:<br />\n<br />\n(1)\treview and update the activities management and specify which type of data (such as audio played on an application and details of past searches) should be saved with your account when using the Services. Further, at the management page, you are able to specify whether certain activities should be stored in Cookies or similar technology on your device when you use the Services while being signed-out from the account. <br />\n<br />\n(2)\treview and edit the advertisement display setting (such as categories you are interested in) relating to advertisements displayed on the Services or on the web page using the advertisement setting. At the advertisement setting page, you are able to select not to use part of the advertisement services provided by the Company. <br />\n<br />\n(3)\tchange the profile associated with your account which is displayed to other users.<br />\n<br />\n(4)\tmanage the persons with whom you can share information through your account.<br />\n<br />\n(5)\tselect to display your profile name or profile photo in the shared recommended information displayed in the advertisements.<br />\n<br />\nFurther, the services offered by the Company provide you with various options with respect to sharing and removing your contents.<br />\n<br />\nWhenever you use the Company’s services, the Company aims to provide you with access to your User Information. If that information is wrong, the Company endeavours to provide you with ways to promptly update it or to delete it unless the Company has to keep such information for legitimate business or legal purposes. When updating your personal information, the Company may ask you to verify your identity before it can act on your request.<br />\n<br />\nThe Company may reject requests that are unreasonably repetitive, require disproportionate technical effort (for example, developing a new system or fundamentally changing an existing practice), risk the privacy of others, or would be extremely impractical (such as requests concerning information stored on the backup system).<br />\n<br />\nWhere the Company can provide access to or correction of User Information, it will do so for free, except where it would require a disproportionate effort.
